# College plans re-marked against the questions

Generated by `build_college_index.py` from `marks/<plan>.md`; edit those. Each part lists,
per topic, the plan row, its old and new mark (Solves, Partly, No, or a `[new-page]` row) and
the record ids that show why, then the taxonomy gaps the records reveal ("Not in the
taxonomy", for `docs/HE_TAXONOMY_GAPS.md`).

## Mathematics

Research group R1, 2026-10-02. The plan's "Answers" rows of `docs/plans/he.math.md` re-marked
against the 634 records of `research/questions/college/math.jsonl`. Every record now carries
`page` (the planned page that should answer it, or null) and `mark` (Solves, Partly, No). Marks
were matched from each record's question type to the plan's rows, then the row was kept or
changed; item ids show why. "Keep" rows list the items only where the evidence is notable.

### Coverage notes

- Thinnest topics: calc-1#4 (5 records), calc-2#5 (6), diff-eq#4 (7), calc-2#1 (9), calc-1#0 (10).
- AP Central serves only 2023–2026: 24 AB and 12 BC-only free-response questions (targets 40 + 40).

### Not in the taxonomy (for docs/HE_TAXONOMY_GAPS.md)

- Calculus I: mean value theorem (OCW-18.01SC-E2-Q6, OCW-18.01SC-F-Q15); Newton's method (OCW-18.01SC-E2-Q5); curve sketching (OCW-18.01SC-E2-Q3, OCW-18.01SC-F-Q4).
- Calculus II: numerical integration (trapezoid, midpoint, Simpson) — OCW-18.01SC-E3-Q5, OCW-18.01SC-F-Q11, AP-CALCAB-2025-Q3, ACS2E-5.6-Ex5, ACS2E-5.6-Ex7; centres of mass and centroids — OCW-18.01SC-E3-Q4, ACS2E-6.3-Ex5, ACS2E-6.3-Ex6, ACS2E-6.3-Ex7; hydrostatic force (Active Calculus 6.4, APEX 7.6).
- Calculus III: cylindrical and spherical coordinates, change of variables — OCW-18.02SC-F-Q12, ACM2E-S-Precalc-OtherCoordSystems-Ex36, ACM2E-S-11-8-Triple-Integrals-Cylindrical-Spherical-Ex21, ACM2E-S-11-8-Triple-Integrals-Cylindrical-Spherical-Ex22, OCW-18.02SC-E3-Q6, OCW-18.02SC-F-Q10.
- Differential Equations: Fourier series (OCW-18.03SC-E3-Q1, OCW-18.03SC-F-Q5) and PDEs (Lebl 4, Trench 11–13); exact equations (LEBL-1.8.2); nonlinear systems and linearization (OCW-18.03SC-F-Q10, LEBL-8.1.1).
- Linear Algebra: linear transformations (as above); positive definite matrices and quadratic forms — OCW-18.06S10-E3-Q2, ULA-quadratic-forms-Ex2; symmetric matrices and the SVD — ULA-symmetric-matrices-Ex1, ULA-symmetric-matrices-Ex3, ULA-svd-intro-Ex1, ULA-svd-intro-Ex2, ULA-svd-intro-Ex3.

## Physics

Research group R1, 2026-10-02. The "Answers" rows of `docs/plans/he.physics.md` re-marked against
the 499 records of `research/questions/college/physics.jsonl`. Every record now carries `page` (the
planned page that should answer it, or null) and `mark` (Solves 44, Partly 210, No 245). Marks were
matched from each record's question (or, for type-only records, its question type) to the plan's
rows, then the row was kept or changed; item ids show why. Many Simple Nature items are
explain-why, estimate or show-that problems: they are marked No against no page, and are listed
once per topic at the end, not as plan rows.

## Chemistry

Written by research group R2 from `research/questions/college/chemistry.jsonl` (390 records). For each topic: the plan's rows ("Tests ask"), the mark the plan gave, the mark the records support, and the record ids. "Kept" means the records agree; a row the plan lacks is added (`[new-page]` when no planned page answers it). Each record's own `page` and `mark` say how far the named page answers that record.

Records: 72 Solves, 214 Partly, 104 No.

### Not in the taxonomy (for `docs/HE_TAXONOMY_GAPS.md`)

Question types the records show that no `he.chemistry.*` topic holds (the plan's own list is
confirmed; the record ids show how often each is asked):

- **Intermolecular forces, liquids and boiling points** (gen chem): AP-CHEM-2023-Q6, AP-CHEM-2025-Q4,
  AP-CHEM-2025-Q5, AP-CHEM-2026-Q5. Every AP year asks it; the Grade 10 page `s.10.molecular-shape`
  is the only home.
- **Solutions: Henry's law, colligative properties, regular solutions** (gen chem II, physical I):
  OCW-5.111-F14-E3-Q5, OCW-20.110-F05-PS7-Q1-2, OCW-20.110-F05-PS7-Q4-5.
- **Solubility equilibria** as a topic (only `gen-chem-2#1~common-ion` and Grade 10): AP-CHEM-2023-Q7,
  AVERILL-17.2-N2, AP-CHEM-2025-Q1, AP-CHEM-2026-Q1.
- **Photoelectron spectroscopy and mass spectra of elements** (gen chem I): OCW-5.111-F08-E1-Q3a,
  AP-CHEM-2025-Q1.
- **Redox balancing and oxidizing/reducing strength** (gen chem II; Grade 10 covers oxidation
  numbers only): OCW-5.111-F14-E4-Q5, OCW-5.111-F14-E4-Q6, AVERILL-19.3-N3.
- **Radical halogenation and its selectivity** (organic I): OCW-5.12-S05-E3-Q9, OCW-5.12-S05-PS5-Q7-9.
- **Reaction mechanisms with curved arrows** (organic I and II, biochemistry): 17 records mark "No" in the mechanism rows
  (no page draws a mechanism) (e.g. OCW-5.13-F06-X3-Q4, OCW-5.12-S05-E4-Q6-7, OCW-5.07SC-F13-PS4-Q1-2).
  Not a taxonomy gap (each topic includes its mechanisms) but the largest uncovered type.
- **Pericyclic reactions, enolate alkylation and condensations, ethers and epoxides** (organic II):
  OCW-5.13-F06-E2-Q1, OCW-5.13-F06-E2-Q2, OCW-5.13-F06-PS7-Q6-14, OCW-5.13-F06-PS8-Q1-6.
- **Structure from combined spectra** (organic I; the plan already lists it): OCW-5.13-F06-E1-Q2,
  -Q3, -Q4, OCW-5.13-F06-F-Q1-2.
- **Statistical thermodynamics** (physical I): OCW-20.110-F05-PS5-Q3-5.
- **Many-electron atoms, perturbation theory, operator algebra** (physical II): OCW-5.61-F17-PS5-Q2-4,
  OCW-5.61-F17-PS7-Q2-3, OCW-5.61-F17-E3-III, OCW-5.61-F17-E1-IV-V, FLEMING-QC-6-P1.
- **Electronic spectra of complexes (Tanabe–Sugano), MO diagrams of complexes, organometallics**
  (inorganic): NOCERA-504-E2-Q3, NOCERA-504-PS3-Q1, NOCERA-504-PS5-Q1, NOCERA-504-E3-Q2.
- **Metabolism beyond the four biochemistry topics** (amino-acid and fatty-acid synthesis, glycogen,
  photosynthesis): OCW-5.07SC-F13-PS9-Q3, OCW-7.05-S20-PS9-Q3, OCW-7.05-S20-PS10-Q4,
  OCW-7.05-S20-PS11-Q1.
- **Analytical methods outside the five topics** (gravimetry, Kovats and size-exclusion, kinetic
  methods): HARVEY-AC2.1-12-P16, HARVEY-AC2.1-12-P28, ICHO-PREP-2018-P10.

## Earth and geography

Research group R3, 2026-10-02. Each plan row ("Asks" tables) re-marked against the recorded items in `research/questions/college/earth-science.jsonl` and `geography.jsonl`. A row keeps its mark when at least one item is solved by its page; it becomes **Partly** when every matched item is only partly answered (the page solves a narrower form); rows with no item keep their mark (no evidence either way). New rows (`[new-page]`) are question types the items show and the plan lacks. Items are matched to a row by their `page` and, where one page carries several rows, by the words of the question type. A row is downgraded only by items that share at least two content words with it (the same question type); looser matches (bridge-level map reading, prose variants) keep the mark and are listed as evidence.

- `geography.physical-geography#1`: why Norway's growing season is shorter than Spain's (NAEP-1994-12G7-#3); how climate differs from weather (NAEP-2018-8G6-#16); what several thematic maps imply about a region's farming (NAEP-2001-12G7-#10)
- `geography.physical-geography#2`: place four named physical features on a hemisphere map (NAEP-1994-12G4-#5); area with the most earthquakes on a world map (NAEP-1994-12G6-#3); draw a self-consistent contour map with saddles, four outflowing streams and their divides (OCW-12.090-MID-Q1)
- `geography.remote-sensing#0`: the obvious energy source and the 'sensor' you use (NRCAN-FRS-1.2-Q1); can reflective and emissive infrared be photographed (NRCAN-FRS-1.3-Q1); why spaceborne radar must be synthetic aperture (NRCAN-FRS-3.3-Q1); brightness temperature from a measured radiance (inverse Planck) (STULL-08-A4); maximum unambiguous range of a radar from its pulse repetition frequency (STULL-08-A13)
- `geography.remote-sensing#1`: satellite or aircraft scanner for mountains (relief displacement) (NRCAN-FRS-2.10-Q1); interpret circular green fields on a Landsat image (centre-pivot irrigation) (NRCAN-FRS-5.2-Q1); shade of grey of each cloud type in visible, IR and water-vapour images (STULL-08-A8)
- `geography.remote-sensing#2`: appearance of flooded cropland on radar (NRCAN-FRS-3.5-Q1)

### Not in the taxonomy

Gaps the items show (for `docs/HE_TAXONOMY_GAPS.md`); each confirms or adds to the plan's own list.

- **Structural geology** (strike and dip, folds, normal and reverse faults): EARLE-RQ12-3, XL-NAEP-2009-12S9-#17; Earle ch. 12 and Johnson ch. 9 teach it. Confirms the plan's gap.
- **Soils** (horizons, soil orders, weathering products): EARLE-EX5.2, NAEP-1994-12G6-#14; Earle ch. 5, Dastrup 5.2. Confirms.
- **Paleoclimate proxies** (δ¹⁸O in ice and foraminifera, core records, isotope mass balance): OCW-12.340-PS1-Q2, OCW-12.340-PS1-Q3, SCHM-3-Q2, SCHM-3-Q5. Confirms.
- **Active remote sensing** (radar range and Doppler, SAR): STULL-08-A13, NRCAN-FRS-3.3-Q1, NRCAN-FRS-3.5-Q1; NRCan chapter 3. Confirms.
- **Severe weather** (tornado risk, hurricane quadrants): NAEP-2018-8G6-#10, NAEP-2018-8G6-#14. Confirms.
- **Political geography and development** (territoriality, sovereignty, redistricting, HDI, Rostow, supranational organizations): AP-HUG-2023-S2-Q1, AP-HUG-2023-S2-Q2, AP-HUG-2024-S1-Q2b, AP-HUG-2025-S1-Q1, AP-HUG-2025-S2-Q3. Confirms; a third of recent AP Human Geography free-response parts sit here.
- **Agriculture and food systems** (hearths of domestication, Green Revolution, carrying capacity, comparative advantage): AP-HUG-2023-S1-Q2, AP-HUG-2024-S1-Q1, AP-HUG-2025-S2-Q2. New: Human Geography's four topics have no agriculture topic (von Thünen sits in economic geography only).
- **Biological oceanography** (primary production): Webb ch. 7 (no items). Confirms.
- **Ocean acoustics** (sound speed, sonar, decibels): OCW-2.011-HW4-P2; Webb 6.4. New, small.
- **Groundwater transport** (advection, sorption retardation, dispersion; transient Theis flow; image wells): OCW-1.72-PS5-Q1, OCW-1.72-FINAL-Q4, OCW-1.72-FINAL-Q6. New: Hydrology #2 is steady Darcy flow only.
- **Atmospheric dynamics beyond geostrophy** (vorticity, thermal wind, Rossby waves, omega): STULL-11-A10, STULL-11-A23, STULL-13-A11, STULL-13-A14, OCW-12.003-PS6-Q3. New: neither Meteorology #3 nor Climatology #1 names them.
- **Mineral physics and crystal chemistry** (radius ratio, coordination, Pauling's rules, optic sign and 2V, hardness scales): OCW-12.108-PS1-Q2, OCW-12.108-PS1-Q3, OCW-12.108-LAB16-Q1, OCW-12.108-LAB12-Q1. New: Mineralogy's three topics leave them out.

## Biology and bioengineering

Written by research group R2 from `research/questions/college/biology.jsonl` and `bio.jsonl` (283 records; the five Bioengineering courses of this plan are field `bio`). For each topic: the plan's "Tests ask" items (a table, or the inline list where the plan has no table), the mark the plan gave, the mark the records support, and the record ids. "Kept" means the records agree; a row the plan lacks is added (`[new-page]` when a new calculator or layout page should answer it; other added rows are types no planned page covers and none is proposed). Each record's own `page` and `mark` say how far the named page answers that record.

Records: 43 Solves, 117 Partly, 123 No.

### Not in the taxonomy (for `docs/HE_TAXONOMY_GAPS.md`)

The plan has no "Not in the taxonomy" section; these are question types the records show that no
`he.biology.*` or bioengineering topic holds:

- **Renal physiology and clearance** (A&P has no kidney topic): OCW-HST.542-S04-PS10-P1-2 (filed under
  `biotransport#3`); Campbell, Marieb and Wakim each have a urinary chapter.
- **Development, stem cells and induction**: OCW-7.013-S18-E3-Q4; 7.016 L24 and L35.
- **Cancer biology** (oncogenes, tumour suppressors, metastasis), filed under `cell-molecular#3`:
  OCW-7.016-F18-E3-Q3, OCW-7.013-S18-E3-Q3.
- **Photosynthesis light reactions**: AP-BIO-2023-Q4 (only `principles-1#2`).
- **Animal behaviour**: AP-BIO-2025-Q2A, -Q2BC, -Q2D (filed under `principles-2#3`).
- **Bacterial and phage gene mapping** (cotransduction, Hfr times): OCW-7.03-2004-E2-Q3, OCW-7.03-2002-E1-Q3,
  OCW-7.03-2002-E2-Q1, OCW-20.106-F06-PS3-Q5.
- **Human linkage (LOD scores)**: OCW-7.03-2004-E3-Q3, OCW-7.03-2000-E3-Q2, OCW-7.03-PS7-Q1.
- **Pharmacology beyond receptors and kinetics** (drug properties, systems drugs): OCW-HST.151-S05-F-Q2-6.
- **Microbial metabolism types and chemostats**: OCW-1.018-F09-E3-Q5, OCW-7.014-S05-Q3-Q1.
- **Surface science of biomaterials** (contact angle, XPS, protein adsorption): OCW-3.051-S06-PS1-Q2,
  -PS3, -PS4, -PS5, OCW-20.441-F09-HW5.
- **Single-molecule and cell mechanics**: OCW-20.310-S15-PS1-P1, -PS1-P2, -PS2-P1-2, -PS6-P1-2.
- **Gait (`biomechanics#2`) and bioreactors (`tissue-engineering#2`)** are in the taxonomy but no
  record reached them (no open source reachable).

## Mechanical

Research group R3, 2026-10-02. Each type in the plan's "Asks" bullets is re-marked against the recorded items in `research/questions/college/classical.jsonl` and `mechanical.jsonl` (716 items). The plan marks every listed type as solved by its page (the Verdict lines), so the old mark is **Solves** throughout. A type keeps it when at least one item is solved by its page; it becomes **Partly** when every item of that type (sharing at least two content words with the row) is only partly answered. Types with no item keep the mark (no evidence). New rows (`[new-page]`) are question types the items show and the plan lacks. In the plan file the changes are written into the Asks bullets ("; re-marked Partly: ids") and the Verdict lines, and the new rows are appended to the Asks bullets; no other plan text was changed.

### Items no page answers (not flagged as new pages)

Types outside the plan's pages that are too far from a calculator to propose a page (derivations, design essays, software steps, multi-body systems), or that a flagged new row already covers; listed so the lesson chat can decide.

- `advanced-solid-mechanics#0`: direction of zero conductivity of a crystal from its tensor (DOIT-TENSORS-5)
- `advanced-solid-mechanics#3`: load a two-bar steel frame carries without yielding (ROY-6.1-15)
- `dynamics#0`: a–t and s–t from a piecewise v–t graph, total distance (MM-C7-4); collar speed when a rope is pulled (MM-C7-9)
- `dynamics#1`: range of a cannonball against a constant headwind (MM-C8-2); tension and angular acceleration as a tether is reeled in (MM-C8-4)

- `dynamics#3`: output speed of a belt-driven pulley train (MM-C11-2); trapdoor angular velocity and acceleration from a cylinder rate (MM-C11-4); velocity and acceleration of a two-link arm's tip (MM-C11-5); wheel normal forces of a braking SUV (MM-C12-1); beam on two cables: accelerations and cable tensions (MM-C12-4); motor torques for a two-link arm (MM-C12-5) …

- `finite-element-analysis#4`: axisymmetric FE solution for a thick vessel (ROY-5.3-5)

- `fluid-mechanics#4`: settling speed of a piston leaking through a thin gap (OCW-2.06-PS5-1); speed of a free plate between two viscous films (OCW-2.06-R5-2)
- `heat-transfer#0`: centre temperature of an aluminium cylinder quenched in a bath (AHTT-5.46); heat from a pipe buried below a surface (AHTT-5.18); conduction through a conical section (OCW-16.050-PS8-3)

### Not in the taxonomy

Gaps the items show (for `docs/HE_TAXONOMY_GAPS.md`); each confirms or adds to the plan's own list.

- **Stress transformation and combined loading** (eccentric axial loads, bending plus torsion, absolute maximum shear): SOM-14.1, SOM-14.2, SOM-14.9, SOM-14.10, SOM-14.11, ROY-3.3-6, SOM-12.39; Strength of Materials ch. 12 and 14 and Roylance 3.3. Confirms the plan's gap (a seventh Mechanics of Materials topic).
- **Equivalent force systems and 3-D statics** (force–couple systems, moment about an axis, 3-D particles and bodies): ES-C4-11 to ES-C4-14, MM-C4-1 to MM-C4-5, MM-C3-8, MM-C2-7, MM-C3-15; a chapter in Engineering Statics (4) and Mechanics Map (4). New: Statics #0 holds them only as types.
- **Kinematics of mechanisms** (four-bar synthesis, belt and gear trains, linkages driven by cylinders): OCW-2.007-E1B-2, MM-C11-2, MM-C11-4, ES-C6-19. Confirms.
- **Polar coordinates and variable-mass or central-force particle problems**: MM-C7-7, MM-C8-4, OCW-2.003SC-PS2-2, OCW-2.003SC-PS4-5. New, small: Dynamics #0 and #1 list polar coordinates as Partly only.
- **Combustion and reacting mixtures** (adiabatic flame temperature): OCW-16.050-PS8-1. New for this group (taught in 16.050 and most thermodynamics texts' last chapters).
- **Compressible and unsteady discharge** (cabin decompression, ramjet): OCW-16.050-PS3-5, OCW-16.050-PS6-6. Confirms (owned by aerospace).
- **Surface tension and capillarity; fluids in rigid-body motion**: OCW-2.06-PS2-1, OCW-2.06-PS2-4, OCW-2.06-PS2-5, OCW-2.06-PS2-6, OCW-2.06-PQ1-1, OCW-2.06-Q1-3, OCW-2.06-PS4-6. New: Fluid statics #0 covers neither, and 2.06 spends two problem sets on them.
- **Viscous flow fields** (divergence and streamlines, Couette and gap flows): OCW-2.06-PS4-5, OCW-2.06-PS5-1, OCW-2.06-R5-2. New, small.
- **Multidimensional and transient conduction** (shape factors, Heisler and one-term solutions): AHTT-2.15, AHTT-5.18, AHTT-5.46, OCW-16.050-PS8-3. Confirms.
- **Plasticity and crystal plasticity in materials** (Schmid factor, slip systems, work hardening, power-law hardening and necking): DOIT-SLIP-1, DOIT-SLIP-4, DOIT-SLIP-6, DOIT-WORK-HARDEN-1, ROY-1.4-4, ROY-1.4-9. New: Materials Science #3 is tensile-test arithmetic only.
- **Fatigue crack growth and creep** (Paris law, creep stages, CTOD and J): ROY-6.5-5, OCW-2.002-PQ2-4, OCW-3.35-PS3-5, DOIT-CREEP-1. Confirms the plan's fracture–creep gap.
- **Polymer processing** (injection moulding pressure, clamp force, cooling time; thermoforming) and **design for assembly**: OCW-2.008-HW1-2, OCW-2.008-Q1-2, OCW-2.008-HW3-1. New: Casting and forming #0 has no polymer process.
- **Finite differences for boundary-value problems; eigenproblems**: KAW-08ode_finitediff-2, KAW-08ode_finitediff-3, OCW-2.092-HW8-4. Confirms.
- **Nonlinear FEA** (snap-through, geometric nonlinearity) and **axisymmetric or 2-D solid elements**: OCW-2.092-HW5-1, ROY-5.3-5, OCW-2.092-Q1-2. Confirms the 2-D element gap; nonlinear analysis is new.
- **Section views and drafting practice** (cutting planes, line conventions, sketching): FDS-ST2-9, OCW-2.007-E1A-7. New, small: CAD #0 lists views and lines but not sections.

### Not in the taxonomy (from the items)

- **Structural Analysis: deflections** (double integration, virtual work): Udoeyo chapters 7–8 and 1.571 PS2 (`UDO-SA-7.1`, `UDO-SA-8.1`, `OCW-1.571-PS2-1`). Confirms the plan’s gap.
- **Compressible Flow: Fanno and Rayleigh flow**: heat addition in a duct (`OCW-16.01-F11`, `OCW-16.01-Q4F-1`); 16.120 lectures 6–8. Confirms the plan’s gap.
- **Soil Mechanics: permeability and seepage; lateral earth pressure; slopes; deep foundations**: Verruijt chapters 6–10, 33–36, 46, 49 (`VER-SM-7.1`, `VER-SM-7.2`, `VER-SM-33.2`, `VER-SM-33.3`), 1.364 (`OCW-1.364-HW3-1`, `OCW-1.364-HW4-2`, `OCW-1.364-HW5`), 1.34 seepage velocity (`OCW-1.34-HW2-3`). Confirms permeability and lateral pressure; adds slope stability and piles.
- **Hydraulics & Hydrology: pumps**: system curve and operating point (`OCW-1.060-PS6-2`, `OCW-1.060-PS6-3`); HEC-22 chapter 9. Confirms.
- **Reaction Engineering: nonisothermal reactors and residence-time distribution**: `OCW-10.37-HW6-1`, `OCW-10.37-MT1-2`, `WOOLF-11.6-EX1`, `OCW-10.450-PS8`. Confirms the nonisothermal gap; adds RTD.
- **Separations: adsorption and chromatography** have no topic: `OCW-10.32-EX3-1`, `OCW-10.32-EX3-2`.
- **Aerospace Structures: structural dynamics** (16.20 units 19–23: `OCW-16.20-HA11-1` … `-3`) and **propulsion: rocket cooling and turbomachinery** (16.50, 16.512) have no topic; **electric propulsion** confirmed missing (`OCW-16.50-HW2`).
- **Orbital Mechanics: Lambert’s problem, rendezvous (Clohessy–Wiltshire) and perturbations** (16.346: `OCW-16.346-EX08`, `-EX26`) are graduate topics outside the four.
- **Environmental: site remediation and risk assessment** (1.34: `OCW-1.34-HW2-1`, `-HW6-3`) sit outside solid waste.
- **Transportation: queueing, vehicle dynamics and transit** are taught with traffic flow (Wikibooks) but have no topic; **pavement design** found no open problems.
- **Surveying**: no open source with problems was reached; the course rests on the plan’s own types.

### Totals

keep 140, new 34, no 162.

## Electrical and computer

Research group R4, 2026-10-02. The plan lists each topic’s question types as "Asks: type → page"; an ask with a page is marked Solves, one with "no page yet" No. Each record in `../electrical.jsonl` and `../computer.jsonl` names the page that answers it (or none). "keep" means at least one item is fully answered by that page; "keep (items ask more)" means every matched item goes past the page (the record says how, mark Partly); "no item" means none was found. New asks come from item types no ask covers; they are added to the plan as "type → [new-page]" (No). Reference only.

### Not in the taxonomy (from the items)

- **Communication Systems: multiple access** (TDMA, Aloha): `OCW-6.02-PS7-1`, `OCW-6.02-PS7-3`.
- **Power Systems: magnetic circuits and electromechanical force** (lift magnets, reluctance): `OCW-6.061-Q2-2`; 6.061 lectures 14–.
- **Data Structures: dynamic programming and shortest-path algorithms** are taught with the course (6.006 lectures 15–20); shortest paths sit in networks#2 only.
- **Digital Logic / Computer Architecture: information and reliability measures** (bits, Huffman codes, MTBF) open 6.004: `OCW-6.004-Q1-1`, `OCW-6.004-Q1-2`, `OCW-6.004-Q2-2`.
- **Discrete Math: number theory and generating functions** (Levin chapter 5) have no topic.
- **Circuits: troubleshooting (diagnostic reasoning)** fills a third of every ModEL module and has no topic.

### Totals

keep 98, new 24, no 178.
